<domain>
(define (domain orchestrator)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types obj)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?o - obj)
    (cats ?o - obj)
    (texture ?o - obj)
    (vase ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
    (next ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
    (sneeze ?o - obj)
    (spring ?o - obj)
    (stupendous ?o - obj)
    (collect ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
  )

  ;; paltry:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, cats ?c, texture ?t, vase ?h ?c, next ?c ?t
  ;; Uniqueness constraint for successor of ?h: require that no next(?h, ?) currently holds
  ;; Effects: add next(?h, ?t), remove vase(?h, ?c)
  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?h - obj ?c - obj ?t - obj ?old - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (next ?c ?t)
      (not (next ?h ?old))
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?c))
    )
  )

  ;; sip:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, cats ?c, texture ?t, next ?h ?t, next ?c ?t
  ;; Additional grounding precondition: require collect(?t, ?col) so vase production is causally grounded
  ;; Effects: add vase(?h, ?c), remove next(?h, ?t)
  (:action sip
    :parameters (?h - obj ?c - obj ?t - obj ?col - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (next ?c ?t)
      (collect ?t ?col)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  ;; clip:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, sneeze ?s, texture ?t, next ?s ?t, next ?h ?t
  ;; Additional causal grounding: require collect(?t, ?col)
  ;; Effects: add vase(?h, ?s), remove next(?h, ?t)
  (:action clip
    :parameters (?h - obj ?s - obj ?t - obj ?col - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (collect ?t ?col)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  ;; wretched:
  ;; Preconditions: sneeze ?x, texture ?t1, texture ?t2, stupendous ?st, next ?x ?t1, collect ?t1 ?st, collect ?t2 ?st
  ;; Effects: add next ?x ?t2, remove next ?x ?t1
  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - obj ?t1 - obj ?t2 - obj ?st - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?t1)
      (texture ?t2)
      (stupendous ?st)
      (next ?x ?t1)
      (collect ?t1 ?st)
      (collect ?t2 ?st)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?t2)
      (not (next ?x ?t1))
    )
  )

  ;; memory:
  ;; Preconditions: cats ?c, spring ?s1, spring ?s2, next ?c ?s1
  ;; Effects: add next ?c ?s2, remove next ?c ?s1
  (:action memory
    :parameters (?c - obj ?s1 - obj ?s2 - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?c)
      (spring ?s1)
      (spring ?s2)
      (next ?c ?s1)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?c ?s2)
      (not (next ?c ?s1))
    )
  )

  ;; tightfisted:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, sneeze ?s, texture ?t, next ?s ?t, vase ?h ?s
  ;; Enforce uniqueness for successor of ?h (no existing next ?h ?old)
  ;; Effects: add next ?h ?t, remove vase ?h ?s
  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?h - obj ?s - obj ?t - obj ?old - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?old))
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?s))
    )
  )

)
</domain>

    (spring object_7)

    (stupendous object_3)

    ;; binary predicates

    (next object_1 object_7)

    (next object_5 object_7)
    (next object_8 object_7)

    (collect object_6 object_2)
    (collect object_7 object_3)

  ))
)

(define (domain orchestrator)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types obj)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?o - obj)
    (cats ?o - obj)
    (texture ?o - obj)
    (vase ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
    (next ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
    (sneeze ?o - obj)
    (spring ?o - obj)
    (stupendous ?o - obj)
    (collect ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
  )

  ;; paltry:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, cats ?c, texture ?t, vase ?h ?c, next ?c ?t
  ;; Uniqueness constraint for successor of ?h: require that no next(?h, ?) currently holds
  ;; Effects: add next(?h, ?t), remove vase(?h, ?c)
  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?h - obj ?c - obj ?t - obj ?old - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (next ?c ?t)
      (not (next ?h ?old))
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?c))
    )
  )

  ;; sip:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, cats ?c, texture ?t, next ?h ?t, next ?c ?t
  ;; Additional grounding precondition: require collect(?t, ?col) so vase production is causally grounded
  ;; Effects: add vase(?h, ?c), remove next(?h, ?t)
  (:action sip
    :parameters (?h - obj ?c - obj ?t - obj ?col - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (next ?c ?t)
      (collect ?t ?col)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  ;; clip:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, sneeze ?s, texture ?t, next ?s ?t, next ?h ?t
  ;; Additional causal grounding: require collect(?t, ?col)
  ;; Effects: add vase(?h, ?s), remove next(?h, ?t)
  (:action clip
    :parameters (?h - obj ?s - obj ?t - obj ?col - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (collect ?t ?col)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  ;; wretched:
  ;; Preconditions: sneeze ?x, texture ?t1, texture ?t2, stupendous ?st, next ?x ?t1, collect ?t1 ?st, collect ?t2 ?st
  ;; Effects: add next ?x ?t2, remove next ?x ?t1
  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - obj ?t1 - obj ?t2 - obj ?st - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?t1)
      (texture ?t2)
      (stupendous ?st)
      (next ?x ?t1)
      (collect ?t1 ?st)
      (collect ?t2 ?st)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?t2)
      (not (next ?x ?t1))
    )
  )

  ;; memory:
  ;; Preconditions: cats ?c, spring ?s1, spring ?s2, next ?c ?s1
  ;; Effects: add next ?c ?s2, remove next ?c ?s1
  (:action memory
    :parameters (?c - obj ?s1 - obj ?s2 - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?c)
      (spring ?s1)
      (spring ?s2)
      (next ?c ?s1)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?c ?s2)
      (not (next ?c ?s1))
    )
  )

  ;; tightfisted:
  ;; Preconditions: hand ?h, sneeze ?s, texture ?t, next ?s ?t, vase ?h ?s
  ;; Enforce uniqueness for successor of ?h (no existing next ?h ?old)
  ;; Effects: add next ?h ?t, remove vase ?h ?s
  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?h - obj ?s - obj ?t - obj ?old - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?old))
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?s))
    )
  )

<domain>
(define (domain multiagent-assembly)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types agent station part phase)

  (:predicates
    (at ?ag - agent ?s - station)
    (part_at ?p - part ?s - station)
    (has ?ag - agent ?p - part)
    (assembled ?p - part)
    (inspected ?p - part)
    (phase_active ?ph - phase)
    (next ?ph1 - phase ?ph2 - phase)
    (assembly_station ?s - station)
    (inspection_station ?s - station)
  )

  ; Agent A: movement
  (:action agentA_move
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?from - station ?to - station)
    :precondition (at ?ag ?from)
    :effect (and (not (at ?ag ?from)) (at ?ag ?to))
  )

  ; Agent B: movement
  (:action agentB_move
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?from - station ?to - station)
    :precondition (at ?ag ?from)
    :effect (and (not (at ?ag ?from)) (at ?ag ?to))
  )

  ; Agent A: pick a part from a storage station
  (:action agentA_pick
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?p - part ?s - station)
    :precondition (and (at ?ag ?s) (part_at ?p ?s) (not (has ?ag ?p)))
    :effect (and (has ?ag ?p) (not (part_at ?p ?s)))
  )

  ; Agent A: assemble a part at an assembly station; allowed only during an active assembly phase
  (:action agentA_assemble
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?p - part ?s - station ?ph - phase)
    :precondition (and (at ?ag ?s) (has ?ag ?p) (assembly_station ?s) (phase_active ?ph))
    :effect (and (assembled ?p) (not (has ?ag ?p)))
  )

  ; Agent A: activate the next phase (e.g., phase2) after required assemblies are complete.
  ; This action enforces that assembly(s) must be done before the next phase becomes active.
  (:action agentA_activate_next_phase
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?from - phase ?to - phase ?p - part)
    :precondition (and (at ?ag base) (phase_active ?from) (next ?from ?to) (assembled ?p))
    :effect (and (phase_active ?to) (not (phase_active ?from)))
  )

  ; Agent B: inspect a part at an inspection station; requires that the part is assembled and the inspection phase is active.
  (:action agentB_inspect
    :parameters (?ag - agent ?p - part ?s - station ?ph - phase)
    :precondition (and (at ?ag ?s) (inspection_station ?s) (assembled ?p) (phase_active ?ph) (not (inspected ?p)))
    :effect (inspected ?p)
  )
)
</domain>

; Problem: multiagent-assembly-instance
; Comment / Auditor corrections applied:
; - Explicit phases (phase1, phase2) and successor relation were added to enforce ordering.
; - Phase activation is modeled as a hard constraint: agentA_activate_next_phase requires the assembly to be completed (assembled ?p) before deactivating phase1 and activating phase2. No post-hoc penalties or quota tokens are used.
; - All preferences were encoded as hard constraints (e.g., inspections cannot occur before activation of phase2).
; - Types and all objects are declared explicitly. No placeholder tokens or implicit resources were added.
; - Actions retain agent-origin prefixes to keep origins distinct.
; ============================================================

(define (problem multiagent-assembly-instance)
  (:domain multiagent-assembly)

  (:objects
    ; agents
    agentA agentB - agent

    ; stations
    base storage assembly_station_1 inspection_station_1 - station

    ; parts
    widget - part

    ; phases
    phase1 phase2 - phase
  )

  (:init
    ; initial agent locations
    (at agentA base)
    (at agentB base)

    ; part location
    (part_at widget storage)

    ; station role declarations
    (assembly_station assembly_station_1)
    (inspection_station inspection_station_1)

    ; initial phase and ordering
    (phase_active phase1)
    (next phase1 phase2)
  )

  ; Goal: the part must be inspected and both agents must end at base.
  (:goal
    (and
      (inspected widget)
      (at agentA base)
      (at agentB base)
      ; ensure phase2 has been activated as part of the process
      (phase_active phase2)
    )
  )
)
</problem>

  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)

  (:predicates

    (inspection_station ?s - station)

================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
Below are the complete PDDL domain and problem files targeted for FastDownwards. They encode a strict, ordered multi-agent workflow integrating a player analysis, a planner draft/skeleton, and an auditor review with mandatory corrections. All natural-language preferences are enforced as hard ordering constraints. No extra objects beyond those derived from the inputs are introduced.

Domain: orchestrator-domain.pddl

(:domain orchestrator-domain
  :requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions
  :types agent step phase artifact
  :predicates
    ; phases and contiguity
    (phase-active ?p - phase)
    (phase-next ?p1 - phase ?p2 - phase)
    ; workflow steps
    (step-done ?s - step)
    ; artifacts and product state
    (artifact-created ?a - artifact)
    (artifact-updated ?a - artifact)
    (skeleton-produced)
    ; auditor outcomes and correction flag
    (corrections-required)
    (finalized)
  ; Player performs initial observation/analysis, must happen in phase successor sequence
  (:action player-analyze
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (not (step-done analysis))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done analysis)
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
  ; Planner produces a structural skeleton based on analysis; must follow analysis and phase order
  (:action planner-produce-skeleton
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (step-done analysis)
      (not (step-done skeleton))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done skeleton)
      (skeleton-produced)
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
  ; Planner creates a full draft plan (artifact) and must follow skeleton production
  (:action planner-draft
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase ?a - artifact)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (step-done skeleton)
      (not (step-done draft))
      (not (artifact-created ?a))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done draft)
      (artifact-created ?a)
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
  ; Auditor reviews the draft; per specification auditor will produce a corrections-required flag (hard constraint)
  (:action auditor-review
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase ?a - artifact)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (step-done draft)
      (artifact-created ?a)
      (not (step-done audit))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done audit)
      ; Auditor report requires corrections as a hard constraint integrated from auditor report
      (corrections-required)
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
  ; Planner applies auditor-required corrections. Because corrections are required, this action is mandatory before finalization.
  (:action planner-apply-corrections
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase ?a - artifact)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (step-done audit)
      (corrections-required)
      (artifact-created ?a)
      (not (step-done corrections))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done corrections)
      ; update the same artifact to reflect corrections (no penalty or bookkeeping shortcuts)
      (artifact-updated ?a)
      (not (corrections-required))
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
  ; Orchestrator (final integrator) must finalize; requires corrections to have been applied
  (:action orchestrator-finalize
    :parameters (?p - phase ?np - phase ?a - artifact)
    :precondition (and
      (phase-active ?p)
      (phase-next ?p ?np)
      (step-done corrections)
      (artifact-updated ?a)
      (not (step-done finalize))
    )
    :effect (and
      (step-done finalize)
      (finalized)
      (not (phase-active ?p))
      (phase-active ?np)
    )
  )
)

Problem: orchestrator-problem.pddl

(:problem orchestrator-problem
  :domain orchestrator-domain
  :objects
    ; Agents (kept as named objects to reflect contributors; actions are explicitly prefixed)
    player - agent
    planner - agent
    auditor - agent
    ; Steps represented as step objects (explicit)
    analysis skeleton draft audit corrections finalize - step
    ; Single artifact representing the plan/draft
    plan - artifact
    ; Phases enumerated with successor links to enforce contiguity and prevent reordering
    ph1 ph2 ph3 ph4 ph5 ph6 - phase
  :init
    ; Contiguous phase ordering (explicit successor links)
    (phase-next ph1 ph2)
    (phase-next ph2 ph3)
    (phase-next ph3 ph4)
    (phase-next ph4 ph5)
    (phase-next ph5 ph6)
    ; Start with phase 1 active
    (phase-active ph1)
    ; Nothing done yet: no step-done predicates are true in the initial state
    ; No artifacts created/updated yet
    ; No corrections required initially; auditor-review will set this deterministically
  :goal
    (and
      ; All mandated terminal conditions must be true
      (step-done analysis)
      (step-done skeleton)
      (step-done draft)
      (step-done audit)
      (step-done corrections)
      (step-done finalize)
      ; Final artifact must exist and be updated to incorporate corrections
      (artifact-created plan)
      (artifact-updated plan)
      ; Finalized flag must be true to indicate completion of the workflow
      (finalized)
      ; Last phase is active to reflect that the workflow progressed through all contiguous phases
      (phase-active ph6)
    )
)

Notes on design decisions (encoded into PDDL)
- Every natural-language ordering or preference (e.g., "analysis before skeleton", "skeleton before draft", "auditor corrections required") is implemented as a hard precondition and/or produced fact so that no plan that violates those constraints is possible.
- Phases are explicit objects (ph1..ph6) and phase-next relations enforce contiguity. Each workflow action consumes the current phase-active and activates exactly the next phase; this prevents reordering or ping-ponging between non-contiguous phases.
- The auditor's report is modeled as deterministically setting the corrections-required predicate during auditor-review; planner-apply-corrections requires that predicate and clears it only by applying corrections. This encodes the auditor corrections as mandatory.
- Actions are prefixed by contributor names (player-, planner-, auditor-, orchestrator-) to keep multi-agent contributions explicit.
- No penalty, token, or post-hoc "pay shortfall" constructs are present; violating required order or corrections is impossible because of strict preconditions.
- The :requirements list contains only :strips, :typing, and :negative-preconditions as requested.

You can run these two files with FastDownwards. If you want a variant that, for example, encodes multiple artifacts, parallel reviewer agents, or conditional branching where the auditor might accept the draft without corrections (instead of the current enforced-corrections model), tell me the allowed alternatives and I will produce an adjusted domain/problem while preserving the hard constraints you requested.
